- **catena**: Catena patch-based version control reference. ALWAYS use this skill when interacting with Catena — running the `catena` CLI, creating/proposing/accepting patches, working with the trunk, releases, reviews, or `.catena` workspaces. Catena is NOT Git: it is online-first and patch-based with a maturity lifecycle (DraftLocal → DraftSynced → DraftShared → Proposed → Accepted). Catena is operated ONLY through the `catena` CLI — never call the server's REST API by hand. Do not assume Git commands, branches, commits, or staging exist.
